**Date:** 2026-09-11 · **Status:** FRESH SOURCE-FIDELITY REVIEW

## Method
Every existing child row in `TRACEABILITY_MATRIX_REQUIREMENTS.md` was re-read. The source-faithful requirement text was normalized and checked against the corresponding immutable RawSourceCorpus. Exact duplicate normalized requirement texts are treated as provenance aliases rather than independent evidence. Broad destination pointers are classified PARTIAL until a precise DD chain exists. DEFERRED/SUPERSEDED remain explicit dispositions. A failed normalized substring check is not automatically called DISTORTED; it is conservatively PARTIAL unless contradictory meaning is proven.
